715 entries, explain-work to explanatory-output-style. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **explain-work**: Explain research, analysis, technical design, or implemented work in terms of stakeholder need, mechanism, tradeoffs, evidence, and project fit. Use for a quick rundown, design walkthrough, stakeholder explanation, or an optional teaching conversation; it does not automatically create a specification or course. ([gcgloven/multiagent-engineer-practices](https://github.com/gcgloven/multiagent-engineer-practices/tree/HEAD/skills/explain-work) · ★4)
- **explain-work**: Review a PR, ticket, or path and explain it so it sticks — mache/CODEOWNERS/ ticket context synthesized into a taught explanation, not a bare findings list. Own work gets onboarding framing; a peer's gets findings-teaching. Not for posting reviews (pr-review) or the reviewer-only pre-flight alone (review-prep). Local only. ([jamestexas/agents](https://github.com/jamestexas/agents/tree/HEAD/skills/explain-work) · ★2)
- **explain-work**: Explain a body of work plainly so a person actually understands it. Runs the `how` and `why` skills and weaves what they find into one clear explanation. Use for 'explain this work to me', 'help me really understand X', 'walk me through this change or subsystem'. ([christinoleo/ak-plugins](https://github.com/christinoleo/ak-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/pstack/explain-work) · ★1)

- **explain-work**: Use when the user asks for a simple, non-technical explanation of what an agent just did, why it was done, what is known vs inferred, where the work stands, whether the session drifted, or what the next step should be.
